While prior keystroke inference techniques have exploited side-channel signals such as acoustics and vibrations, they typically rely on conspicuous, short-range sensors and require victim-specific data for model training, limiting their practicality, scalability, and stealth. In this paper, we present RadKey, an RF backscatter system for covert, long-range, through-wall keystroke eavesdropping. RadKey comprises two components: a compact batteryless backscatter tag and an RF reader. The tag captures keystroke-induced vibrations and acoustic signals, modulating them onto the frequency shift of its backscattered RF signal using two magnetically-coupled LC resonators. This design also enables spectral separation between the excitation and backscatter signals, mitigating self-interference for the RF reader and thus extending eavesdropping range. The RF reader demodulates the backscattered RF signal to infer typed content. It employs a dedicated signal processing pipeline that extracts user- and keyboard-independent keystroke features across time and frequency domains, enabling strong generalizability. To further enhance adaptability, RadKey integrates an LLM for online adaptation, leveraging LLM outputs as pseudo ground-truth labels to refine the classifier during runtime. We have built a prototype of the full RadKey system and evaluated it through extensive over-the-air experiments. Results show that RadKey achieves accurate and robust keystroke inference across diverse users in real-world settings. A demo video is available at: https://radkey-submission.github.io/RadKey/

</details>


### [74] [GRAFT: Graphlet-Triggered Backdoor Attack on GNN-Based Hardware Security Systems](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.10163)
*Sanaz Kazemi Abharian,Sai Manoj Pudukotai Dinakarrao*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 本文提出GRAFT——一种基于图元的后端门攻击方法，针对GNN-based硬件安全系统（硬件木马检测与IP盗版检测），通过在RTL或门级嵌入图元触发器并保持电路功能不变，在ISCAS-85和TrustHub数据集上实现了高达100%的攻击成功率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有GNN后门攻击多采用随机子图或梯度引导生成子图作为触发器，但这些触发器无法保证电路功能的完整性，因此不适用于硬件安全场景。

Method: 提出GRAFT方法，利用图元（graphlet）作为触发器嵌入到电路的RTL级或门级网表中，同时保持电路原始功能不变。

Result: 在ISCAS-85和TrustHub数据集上，GRAFT在硬件木马检测和IP盗版检测任务中均实现了高达100%的攻击成功率（ASR）。

Conclusion: GRAFT证明了基于图元的触发器可以有效地使GNN-based硬件安全系统失效，且不破坏电路功能，揭示了此类系统面临的后端门攻击风险。

Abstract: The globalization of the integrated circuit (IC) supply chain increases the risk of security threats, such as hardware Trojans (HTs) and the theft of intellectual property (IP). Graph Neural Networks (GNNs), among the most powerful deep learning methods for processing graph-structured data, have been widely adopted to detect such threats. However, GNNs are susceptible to backdoor attacks that can maliciously manipulate output predictions toward an adversarial target. These attacks are not only difficult to detect but also compromise the integrity of GNN-based security systems. Most prior work embeds backdoor triggers using randomly generated subgraphs or gradient-guided generative subgraphs. However, such triggers are impractical for GNN-based hardware security applications as they do not guarantee the preservation of circuit functionality. In this paper, we propose GRAFT, a graph let-triggered backdoor attack targeting GNN-based hardware security. GRAFT embeds graphlet-based triggers at either the register-transfer level (RTL) or gate level of the design while preserving the circuit 's original function. We evaluate GRAFT on the ISCAS-85 and TrustHub datasets. Our experimental results demonstrate that GRAFT can effectively evade HT detection and IP piracy detection, achieving an attack success rate (ASR) of up to 100%.

</details>


### [75] [Proof of Source of Funds: Efficient On-chain Provenance of Cryptoassets](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.10172)
*Alireza Kavousi,István András Seres,Zhipeng Wang*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种名为"资金原产地证明"(PoSoF)的全新密码学框架，将去中心化金融中的合规负担从平台端转移到用户端。用户本地生成零知识证明，证明其存款仅来自合规来源，平台只需常数时间O(1)验证即可。方案基于统一的时间DAG抽象和增量可验证计算(IVC)，以太坊原型验证显示：每次交易更新约1.8秒，链上EVM验证仅需约1.5毫秒（~800k gas）。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有监管合规方案依赖于平台的二元准入名单或中心化区块链情报公司的追溯图分析，这不仅剥夺了诚实用户的金融隐私，导致误报与漏报，还强迫去中心化平台承担沉重的链上交易监控负担。作者希望将合规范式从"平台端监控"转向"用户端溯源"，在不泄露隐私的前提下实现合规。

Method: 1) 提出统一的时间DAG抽象，统一建模UTXO和账户模型下的价值流转历史。2) 用户从自身交易历史中提取合规子DAG。3) 利用增量可验证计算(IVC)证明严格的状态转换谓词，对抗多种攻击向量。4) 零知识证明确保不泄露中间交易拓扑、中间地址或具体来源。

Result: 以太坊兼容原型基准测试表明：用户每次新交易后增量更新PoSoF约需1.8秒；最终的链上EVM验证为常数时间，约1.5毫秒（~800k gas）。

Conclusion: 完全隐私保护的主动合规方案是高度实用的，通过将合规负担转移给用户并利用密码学证明，实现了隐私保护与监管合规的双赢。

Abstract: Regulatory compliance is increasingly mandatory for decentralized finance and privacy-enhancing technologies. Current approaches rely on binary inclusion/exclusion lists or retroactive graph analysis by centralized blockchain intelligence firms. This approach strips honest users of their financial privacy, leads to false positives and negatives, and forces decentralized platforms to bear the burden of on-chain transaction monitoring. In this work, we propose a paradigm shift: moving from platform-side surveillance to user-side provenance. We introduce Proof of Source of Funds (PoSoF), a novel cryptographic framework that shifts the burden to the user. Rather than the platform tracing funds, the user locally generates a zero-knowledge proof demonstrating that their deposit originates exclusively from a set of compliant sources. The platform is thus relieved of chain-analysis duties, requiring a constant-time, O(1) verification to enforce admission control.
  We formulate a unified temporal Directed Acyclic Graph (DAG) abstraction that formalizes both UTXO and account-based ledger histories within a generalized value-flow model. Users extract a compliant sub-DAG of their transaction history and utilize Incrementally Verifiable Computation (IVC) to prove rigorous state-transition predicates that protect against various attack vectors. Crucially, PoSoF provides verifiable cryptographic provenance; it guarantees the legitimacy of the funds without leaking the intermediate transaction topology, intermediary addresses, or the specific origins utilized. We formally define the security properties of PoSoF and evaluate an Ethereum-compatible prototype. Our benchmarks demonstrate that fully private, proactive compliance is highly practical, requiring only ~1.8 s to incrementally update a user's PoSoF per new transaction, and a constant-time ~1.5 ms (~800k gas) for final on-chain EVM verification.

</details>


### [76] [Local Is Not a Sufficient Privacy Boundary: Governing OS-Integrated On-Device AI](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.10173)
*Jonghyun Chung,Sanket Badhe*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 本文提出一个以操作系统为中心的隐私框架，强调本地AI的隐私问题不仅是计算位置问题，更是机构问责问题，并通过苹果、安卓和微软的案例进行对比分析。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有研究过度关注"本地推理是否更隐私"，而忽视了AI系统在操作系统中运行时涉及的信息流、权限、持久化状态、遥测和云路由等更广泛的隐私问题，缺乏系统化的分析框架。

Method: 构建了一个包含威胁模型、六部分隐私风险分类、隐私架构控制及四级审计评估框架的方法论，并通过文档对比分析了Apple Intelligence、Android AICore/Gemini Nano和Microsoft Recall三个实际系统。

Result: 研究表明，有意义的隐私取决于受限信息流、有限授权、可见的用户控制以及跨操作系统生命周期的可审计治理机制。

Conclusion: 本地AI的隐私问题应从机构问责角度重新理解，而非简单归结为部署位置问题，需要从操作系统层面建立全面的隐私治理体系。

Abstract: As AI systems move into operating systems, privacy no longer turns only on whether a model runs locally. A local assistant may assemble email, calendar entries, files, screenshots, notifications, and app intents; retain embeddings or summaries; invoke tools; emit telemetry; or route difficult requests to cloud infrastructure. Local inference reduces some exposure, but it answers only one question: where computation occurs. It does not answer who may assemble context, what derived state persists, which actions are authorized, or how updates change the system's authority. We develop an OS-centered privacy framework for on-device AI that treats privacy as an institutional accountability problem rather than a deployment attribute. The framework specifies a threat model, a six-part privacy risk taxonomy, privacy-by-architecture controls, and a four-level audit rubric. We demonstrate the rubric through a documentation-bounded comparison of Apple Intelligence/Foundation Models, Android AICore/Gemini Nano, and Microsoft Recall. Meaningful privacy in on-device AI depends on constrained information flow, bounded authority, visible user control, and auditable governance across the operating-system lifecycle.

</details>


### [77] [RECON: An LLM-Enhanced Backward Constraint Analysis Framework](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.10264)
*Babangida Bappah,Lamine Noureddine,Umar Farooq,Aisha Ali-Gombe*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 提出了一种LLM增强的后向约束分析框架RECON，结合静态程序分析的精确性与LLM的语义理解，从Android字节码中提取精确的执行约束。相比传统符号执行，速度快5.8倍，成功率达100%，在恶意软件分析中约束生成成功率达84%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统符号执行等方法虽为程序分析中的精确约束推理提供了原则性基础，但由于路径爆炸、函数建模需求以及底层程序表示中语义意图的丢失，难以扩展到现代软件系统。在Android这类复杂执行环境中，这些局限更为突出。

Method: RECON框架：从目标方法向后进行路径发现至应用入口点，发现方法级控制流约束，并利用LLM推理将字节码条件转换为可解释的规约。

Result: 使用5种LLM在78个Android约束提取场景中评估：比传统符号执行快5.8倍，成功率100%，输出逻辑等价且更精确可解释。在100个恶意软件样本上，生成语义约束成功率达84%，能检测跨多执行路径的复杂约束。

Conclusion: RECON成功融合了静态程序分析的精确性与LLM的语义理解能力，有效克服了传统符号执行在Android复杂执行环境中的局限性，在效率和精度上均显著优于传统方法。

Abstract: While traditional techniques, such as symbolic execution, provide a principled foundation for precise constraint reasoning in program analysis, they struggle to scale to modern software systems mainly due to path explosion, the need for function modeling, and the loss of semantic intent at low-level program representations. In complex execution environments such as Android, characterized by extensive framework interactions and event-driven behavior, these limitations are even more amplified. Thus, in this paper, we present a novel large language model (LLM)-enhanced backward constraint analysis framework that combines the precision of static program analysis with LLM's semantic understanding to extract precise execution constraints from Android bytecode. Our approach, titled RECON, performs backward path discovery from target method(s) to the application entry point(s), discovers method-level control-flow constraints, and leverages LLM reasoning to transform bytecode conditions into interpretable specifications. We evaluated RECON using five LLMs across 78 Android constraint-extraction scenarios and compared it with traditional symbolic execution on real-world applications. Results demonstrate that our approach operates 5.8X faster than traditional symbolic execution, with a 100% success rate, while maintaining logical equivalence and providing significantly more precise and interpretable output. We further evaluated RECON for malware analysis on 100 samples. The results indicate an 84% success rate in generating semantic constraints that lead to the execution of dangerous API behaviors and in detecting complex constraints across multiple execution paths.

</details>


### [78] [Benchmarking and Exploring the Capabilities of LLMs for Attack Investigations](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.10281)
*Aniket Anand,Yiwei Hou,Daniel Fields,Alex Kantchelian,David Tao,Kurt Thomas,Grant Ho*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 本文提出 AuditBench，一个用于评估 LLM 在安全系统审计日志调查方面能力的新基准数据集，涵盖 Linux/Windows 平台 50+ 安全调查场景，并对五种前沿 LLM 进行了系统评估与分析。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的 LLM 安全应用研究缺乏针对系统审计日志调查任务的标准化评估基准，安全运营团队需要了解 LLM 在真实调查任务（如告警分类、持久化机制识别等）上的表现与局限性。

Method: 构建 AuditBench 基准数据集，包含来自 Linux 和 Windows 系统、涵盖 50 多个恶意与良性安全调查场景的审计日志；设计四项日志调查任务（告警分类、攻击溯源、持久化识别等）；对五种前沿 LLM 进行系统评估，分析模型大小、数据表示、提示构建、任务类型等因素对性能和错误模式的影响。

Result: 揭示了 LLM 在不同设计选择下的性能差异和错误分布特征，包括模型规模、数据表示方式、提示构建策略以及具体调查任务对结果的影响；刻画了 LLM 生成解释的质量及其在基准上的错误类型。

Conclusion: AuditBench 为评估 LLM 在安全日志调查中的能力提供了标准化基础，为安全运营从业者使用 LLM 提供了实践洞见，并为未来研究方向指明了路径。

Abstract: This paper presents AuditBench, a new benchmark dataset for evaluating the capabilities of LLMs at investigating security-related system audit logs. We design and use this benchmark to explore the performance of LLMs on four log-investigation tasks that incident response teams commonly perform, ranging from triaging alerts generated by detectors to identifying persistence mechanisms on compromised systems. AuditBench consists of system audit logs collected from Linux and Windows machines, and spans over 50 different security investigation scenarios, including both malicious and benign activity. Using our benchmark, we evaluate and analyze the performance of five frontier LLMs at analyzing audit logs for attack investigations. Our analysis illuminates how LLM performance and error profiles vary according to different design choices, such as differences in model size, data representation, prompt construction, and specific investigation tasks. Additionally, we characterize the quality of the explanations produced by LLMs and the types of errors that models make across our benchmark. Collectively, our work provides a foundation for assessing the capabilities of LLMs for investigating security logs, novel insights for practitioners using LLMs in security operations, and important directions for future research.

</details>


### [79] [The Linux IOCTL Census: A Source-Derived Database of the Linux Kernel Control-Code Surface](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.10290)
*Michael J. Bommarito*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 本文提出了Linux IOCTL普查系统，通过源代码分析构建了ioctl攻击面的可查询清单，覆盖586个调度入口、1289个命令代码、3583个受控输入接收点和1298个权限门，并回测了22个近期CVE。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: ioctl处理程序是Linux内核中攻击面最广泛且最不统一的局部攻击面之一，因为驱动程序自行定义命令和解析参数，缺乏集中编目，导致存在信任未验证参数长度而越界读写内核内存的风险。

Method: 对allmodconfig构建的878个模块执行确定性libclang单次遍历，恢复ioctl调度入口点、解码命令代码、识别受控输入接收点和权限门；将内核文档化的威胁模型编码为可查询列，分离无能力门的ioctl表面。

Result: 覆盖169个子树的878个模块，恢复586个ioctl调度入口点、1289个解码命令代码、3583个受控输入接收点和1298个权限门，回测22个近期in-tree ioctl CVE，发布了与Windows IOCTL Census共享模式的结构化开放数据。

Conclusion: 该普查系统能够量化Linux内核ioctl攻击面，区分有权限门保护和无权限门暴露的部分，并支持跨操作系统的联合查询。

Abstract: The ioctl system call is Linux's catch-all device-control interface. A userspace program opens a device node and hands the driver a numeric command code and an argument buffer, and the driver does whatever that code means, whether configuring hardware, reading back state, or moving data into and out of the kernel. Drivers define these commands themselves, by the thousand, and parse their arguments in kernel context, which makes ioctl handlers one of the broadest and least uniform local attack surfaces in the kernel. A handler that trusts an argument length it never validates can read or write kernel memory out of bounds, and the command space is catalogued in no central place. We present the Linux IOCTL Census, a source-derived and queryable inventory of that surface. An allmodconfig build compiles 878 modules across 169 subtrees, and over them a single deterministic libclang pass over the kernel source recovers 586 ioctl dispatch entry points, 1,289 decoded _IOC command codes, 3,583 controlled-input sinks, and 1,298 permission gates. A second pass encodes the kernel's own documented threat model as a queryable column, separating the capability-ungated ioctl surface, an upper bound on unprivileged reach rather than proven reach, from the part a hard capability gate puts out of scope. We backtest the census against 22 recent in-tree ioctl CVEs and release the structural tier as open data, on a schema shared with the companion Windows IOCTL Census so a single query spans both operating systems.

</details>


### [80] [Game-Theoretic Multi-Agent Control for Robust Contextual Reasoning in LLMs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.10322)
*Saeid Jamshidi,Amin Nikanjam,Arghavan Moradi Dakhel,Kawser Wazed Nafi,Foutse Khomh*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 本文提出GT-MCP，一种基于博弈论的安全模型上下文协议，通过多智能体控制器联合评估因果一致性、语义一致性和分布漂移，并结合回滚自愈机制，有效抵御多轮交互中的提示注入和上下文投毒攻击，在500轮对抗测试中实现99.6%的上下文稳定性，且计算开销可控。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有LLM多轮交互中的防御方法主要过滤单轮输出，忽略跨轮上下文演化，导致长程推理易受提示注入和上下文投毒攻击；MCP协议仅作为被动路由层，未强制执行上下文稳定性。

Method: GT-MCP将上下文管理建模为闭环动态过程，协调三个异质LLM智能体，通过信任函数联合评估因果一致性（基于验证过的上下文图）、智能体间语义一致性和随时间变化的分布漂移；检测到不稳定时启动基于回滚的自愈机制，恢复已验证上下文并阻止未受支持的片段传播。

Result: 在自适应对抗威胁模型下500轮交互中：99.6%轮次上下文漂移有界，仅0.4%需恢复；每轮效用高度集中（中位数-0.19，P05=-0.72，P95=0.30），严重退化仅0.4%；控制器层面无注入成功；选定输出胜率稳定在98%以上；每token延迟1.63e-3秒，计算开销可预测。

Conclusion: GT-MCP通过多智能体博弈论框架有效保障了多轮LLM交互的上下文安全性，在极高稳定性和极低恢复率之间取得平衡，且计算开销在实际可接受范围内。

Abstract: Large Language Models (LLMs) in multi-turn interactions maintain evolving context rather than generating isolated responses, making them vulnerable to prompt-injection and context-poisoning attacks in which locally plausible adversarial fragments gradually distort reasoning trajectories. Existing defenses mainly filter individual outputs and often ignore context evolution across turns, leaving long-horizon reasoning exposed. Although the Model Context Protocol (MCP) standardizes context exchange and tool invocation, it functions as a passive routing layer and does not enforce contextual stability. To address these limitations, we introduce the Game-Theoretic Secure Model Context Protocol (GT-MCP), a controller-driven multi-agent method that treats context management as a closed-loop dynamical process. GT-MCP coordinates three heterogeneous LLM agents and selects outputs through a trust function that jointly evaluates causal consistency against a validated context graph, semantic agreement among agents, and distributional drift over time. When instability is detected, a rollback-based self-healing mechanism restores the validated context and prevents unsupported fragments from propagating. Empirical evaluation over 500 interaction turns under an adaptive adversarial threat model shows that contextual drift remains bounded in 99.6% of turns, with recovery required in only 0.4%. Per-turn utility remains tightly concentrated, with median = -0.19, P05 = -0.72, and P95 = 0.30; severe degradation below -1 occurs in only 0.4% of cases, and no injection attempt succeeds at the controller level. Selected outputs maintain stable win rates above 98%, and computational overhead remains predictable, with latency per token = 1.63e-3 s.

</details>


### [81] [Semantic Multi-Agent Intrusion Detection for IoT:Zero-Day and Adversarial Threats with Risk-Aware Reasoning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.10323)
*Saeid Jamshidi*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 提出一种语义多智能体入侵检测系统，集成Scout、Mutator、Auditor和Arbiter四个智能体，通过语义嵌入和多阶段概率决策融合，在IoT数据集上实现95.9%检测准确率、6.8%误报率、87.9%零日攻击检测率，并具备边缘部署的计算效率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统入侵检测系统难以泛化到未知攻击、计算资源需求大、缺乏可解释性，尤其在资源受限的异构IoT网络中问题突出。现有深度学习和LLM方法通常仅聚焦零日或对抗性威胁，很少结合语义推理与多智能体系统。

Method: 提出语义多智能体入侵检测系统，包含四个专用智能体：Scout从语义嵌入中诱导结构化假设；Mutator生成对抗性约束变体；Auditor评估一致性并过滤不可靠输出；Arbiter生成可解释的、风险感知的告警。系统采用语义嵌入与多阶段概率决策融合机制。

Result: 在多个真实IoT数据集上，系统实现95.9%整体检测准确率，误报率降至6.8%，零日攻击检测率达87.9%，并保持适合边缘部署的计算效率。

Conclusion: 语义多智能体框架有效结合了语义推理与多智能体协作，在检测精度、零日攻击识别、可解释性和计算效率方面均优于传统方法，适合IoT边缘部署场景。

Abstract: The rapid proliferation of Internet of Things (IoT) devices has enabled unprecedented automation and connectivity, but it has also substantially increased the attack surface, exposing networks to sophisticated cyber threats, including zero-day and adversarial intrusions. Traditional Intrusion Detection Systems (IDS) struggle to generalize to unseen attacks, often require substantial computational resources, and lack interpretability, particularly in resource-constrained and heterogeneous IoT networks. Recent advances, including Deep Learning (DL), open-set detection, and Large Language Model (LLM)-based semantic reasoning, address some of these challenges but typically focus on zero-day and adversarial threats and rarely combine semantic reasoning with multi-agent systems. To overcome these limitations, we propose a semantic multi-agent ID that integrates four specialized agents (e.g., Scout, Mutator, Auditor, and Arbiter) that leverage semantic embeddings and multi-stage probabilistic decision fusion. The Scout induces structured hypotheses from semantic embeddings; the Mutator generates adversarially constrained variants; the Auditor evaluates consistency and filters unreliable outputs; and the Arbiter produces interpretable, risk-aware alerts. Extensive experiments across multiple real-world IoT datasets demonstrate that the proposed system achieves 95.9% overall detection accuracy, reduces false-positive rates to 6.8%, improves zero-day detection to 87.9%, and maintains computational efficiency suitable for edge deployment.

</details>


### [82] [The Distributed Detectability Band Against Marginal-Preserving Attacks](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.10456)
*Zhang Qinqin,Gao Yuze*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 本文提出一种基于高斯copula AR(1)的分布式破坏攻击，能保持每步边际分数与正常行为完全相同，从而欺骗基于边际分布的监测器（Monitor A），而伤害编码在时间相关性结构中。实验表明监测器A的AUC仅0.52（随机水平），而基于时序相关性的监测器（Monitor B）AUC达0.79-0.97，揭示了亚阈值破坏的前沿问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有AI控制监测器仅检查单步动作以检测异常行为，但真实危害可能分布在多个看似正常的步骤中，每个步骤单独低于任何报警阈值。作者旨在构造一种能保持每步边际分数不变的隐蔽攻击，并系统探索这种攻击的可实现性、不同监测器下的表现以及可检测性边界。

Method: 使用高斯copula AR(1)模型构造边际保持、相关性编码的分布式破坏攻击。通过三个评审强制关卡进行验证：(1)可实现性门：攻击的KS距离仅0.013（接近零），伤害水平高达3.0；(2)监测器A与B调和：形式化证明针对监测A（基于边际分数）构造的攻击在监测B（基于时序相关性，如CUSUM、SPRT、HMM-LR、游程检验、自相关、窗口逻辑回归）下仍保持边际不变性；(3)非空可检测带：对比两类监测器在1%假阳性率下的AUC表现。

Result: 攻击在所有测试伤害水平下（最高3.0）KS距离仅0.013（实际为零），确认可实现性不受伤害限制。监测器A的AUC为0.52（随机水平）；监测器B的AUC在0.79-0.97之间。随危害在更多步骤上摊销，监测器A降至随机水平，而监测器B保持约0.95的AUC。

Conclusion: 存在非空可检测带，表征了亚阈值破坏的前沿：基于分布形状的监测器在构造上必然失效；基于时序相关性的监测器可以检测但并非平凡最优。

Abstract: AI-control monitors score individual agent actions to detect misbehavior, but real harm can be distributed across many benign-looking steps, each individually below any per-step alarm. We construct a marginal-preserving, correlation-encoded distributed-sabotage attack using a Gaussian-copula AR(1) construction: the per-step monitor-score marginal is held exactly equal to benign, so mean, max, top-k tail, and threshold monitors (Monitor A) are defeated by construction, while harm is encoded in the temporal correlation structure. We sequence the paper around three reviewer-mandated gates. (1) Realizability gate: the stealthy attack achieves KS-distance to benign of 0.013 (effectively zero) at all tested harm levels up to 3.0, confirming that harm is fully decoupled from the per-step marginal and realizability is not harm-limited. (2) Monitor-A-vs-B reconciliation: we show formally that the attack, built against Monitor A's score marginal, remains marginal-preserving under a different-score Monitor B (the correlation/sequence family: CUSUM, SPRT, HMM-LR, runs test, autocorrelation, windowed logistic), and scope worst-case claims to score functions that admit a temporal signature. (3) Non-empty detectability band: Monitor A achieves AUC 0.52 (chance); Monitor B spans AUC 0.79-0.97 at the same 1% FPR target, and as harm is amortized over more steps Monitor A collapses to chance while Monitor B holds at AUC ~0.95. These results demonstrate a non-empty detectability band and characterize the sub-threshold sabotage frontier: distribution-shape monitors fail by construction; temporal-correlation monitors can detect but are not trivially optimal.

</details>


### [83] [HE-DAP: Homomorphic Encryption-based Dynamic Adaptive Parameter Optimization for Statistical Computation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.10477)
*Yun-Soo Park,Hyunmin Choi,Hyoungshick Kim,Mun-Kyu Lee*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: HE-DAP是一个跨平台优化框架，通过自动平衡多项式次数与迭代次数之间的权衡，在保持高数值精度的前提下，将同态加密中逆平方根计算的性能提升高达2.35倍，从而提升隐私保护统计分析的端到端效率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 同态加密（HE）虽然支持隐私保护分析，但计算开销过高。其中逆平方根计算作为统计和机器学习中的关键原语，在不同HE库和硬件上的性能表现不一致且次优，根源在于高次Chebyshev多项式与bootstrapping之间存在核心权衡，而两者的相对成本在不同环境下差异可达6倍，导致任何固定配置都无法达到最优效率。

Method: 提出HE-DAP跨平台优化框架，通过分析环境的性能特征，自动在多项式次数与迭代次数之间寻找最优平衡点，以在满足给定精度目标的前提下加速加密逆平方根计算。该方法通过对运行环境进行性能剖析（profiling），自适应地确定最优参数配置。

Result: 在Lattigo、HEaaN-CPU和HEaaN-GPU上的评估显示：HE-DAP将逆平方根核心计算加速高达2.35倍（相比PP-STAT的固定配置），同时保持高数值精度（MRE ≤ 3.1×10⁻⁸）。优化该基础构建块直接提升了复杂统计分析任务的端到端性能。

Conclusion: 通过自动适应异构执行环境，HE-DAP证明了原则性的参数优化可以使隐私保护的统计分析在实际规模下变得实用。

Abstract: Homomorphic encryption (HE) enables privacy-preserving analytics but remains hindered by high computational overhead. We find that the inverse square root-a key primitive in many statistical and machine learning workloads-exhibits inconsistent and often suboptimal performance across HE libraries and hardware. This stems from a core trade-off between two costly HE operations: evaluating high-degree Chebyshev polynomials to speed up Newton's method versus performing bootstrapping to manage ciphertext noise. Because their relative costs vary by up to 6x across environments, any fixed configuration proves inherently inefficient.
  To address this challenge, we present HE-DAP, a cross-platform optimization framework that automatically navigates this trade-off. By profiling an environment's unique performance characteristics, HE-DAP finds the optimal balance between polynomial degree and iteration count to accelerate the encrypted inverse square root computation for a given accuracy target. Our evaluation on Lattigo, HEaaN-CPU, and HEaaN-GPU shows that HE-DAP's adaptive approach yields significant performance gains. It accelerates the core inverse square root computation by up to 2.35x over the fixed configuration in PP-STAT while maintaining high numerical accuracy (MRE <= 3.1 x 10^-8). We further demonstrate that optimizing this fundamental building block directly enhances the end-to-end performance of complex statistical analyses, confirming the practical benefits of our environment-aware approach. By automatically adapting to heterogeneous execution environments, HE-DAP demonstrates that principled parameter optimization can make privacy-preserving statistical analytics practical at scale.

</details>


### [84] [AgentCanary: A Security Evaluation Framework for Autonomous AI Agents in Real Executable Environments](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.10484)
*Peiyang Li,Songping Wang,Yi Huang,Yanhua Shi,Chenhao Zhang,Qi Li,Yueming Lyu,Caifeng Shan,Fengting Li,Chao Feng,Chuanqun Zhu,Liang Chen*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: AgentCanary是一个用于自主AI代理的全面安全评估框架，通过正交的Entry×Impact风险分类、高保真可执行环境和轨迹驱动的多维评估，揭示当前代理在面对复杂攻击时安全缺陷严重，特别是在技能受损、持久状态和长程执行攻击场景下。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有安全评估工作存在三大瓶颈：风险覆盖碎片化、执行环境静态或低保真、评估指标单一粗粒度，无法有效应对自主AI代理从对话到任务执行转变带来的系统性安全风险。

Method: 提出三维贡献：1) Entry×Impact正交风险分类体系及场景化任务套件；2) 高保真真实可执行环境（动态预置任务工件、持久状态支持多步交互）；3) 基于完整代理轨迹的多维评估（结果安全性、安全感知、任务效用）。

Result: 在多个前沿模型和三种代理框架上，针对多种对抗攻击方法的评估表明，当前代理经常无法识别其所面临的攻击，尤其是在技能受损、持久状态和长时域执行攻击场景下。

Conclusion: AgentCanary提供了一个系统化的安全评估基线，揭示了现有自主AI代理在安全方面的严重不足，为开发更可靠和安全的代理系统奠定了基础。

Abstract: Autonomous AI agents have driven the transition from conversation to task execution, shifting security failures from textual deception to system compromise. Although security evaluation is crucial for proactive risk prevention, prior work is constrained by fundamental bottlenecks, including fragmented risk coverage, static or low-fidelity execution environments, and single-dimensional and coarse-grained assessment metrics. To address these challenges, we propose AgentCanary, a comprehensive security evaluation framework for autonomous AI agents. AgentCanary provides a systematic solution along three contributions. First, comprehensive risk coverage: we introduce an orthogonal Entry $\times$ Impact risk taxonomy that decouples how adversarial influence enters the agent from what harm it ultimately causes, and instantiate it as a scenario-aligned task suite spanning realistic deployment workflows. Second, a high-fidelity real executable environment: rather than static Q&A or mocked tool responses, agents interact with real tools against dynamically provisioned task artifacts, with persistent state across multi-step interactions that naturally supports long-horizon attack evaluation. Third, trajectory-grounded multi-dimensional evaluation: evaluation consumes the full agent trajectory rather than the reply text or a single tool call, enabling decomposed scoring along three orthogonal dimensions, Outcome Safety, Security Awareness, and Task Utility. We evaluate a broad set of frontier models on AgentCanary against multiple established adversarial attack methods across three agent frameworks. The results reveal that current agents often fail to recognize the attacks they face, particularly under compromised skills, persistent state, and long-horizon execution attacks, and provide a systematic baseline for developing more reliable and secure agent systems.

</details>


### [85] [When VR Meets BCI: (Un)Observable Brainwave-aware Privacy Reconstruction in the Metaverse via Unrestricted Inbuilt Motion Sensors](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.10502)
*Tao Ni,Zehua Sun,Qingchuan Zhao,Wei-Bin Lee,Cong Wang*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 本文提出BraVeSpy系统，利用VR头显内置运动传感器（如加速度计）捕获瞳孔反应引起的细微振动，首次在元宇宙中实现从不可观察的脑电波相关表征重建感知图像（准确率52.0%-67.2%），并超越现有粗粒度可观察行为推理方法，在用户活动推断（85%+）、去匿名化、眼动追踪和虚拟按键推断（96%+）等任务上取得显著效果。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有VR隐私泄露研究仅限于可观察的虚拟场景行为（如用户在看什么），忽略了通过运动传感器重建不可观察的脑电波相关表征（如用户正在感知什么）这一潜在攻击向量。

Method: 利用VR头显内置运动传感器捕捉由瞳孔反应引起的细微振动，设计并实现BraVeSpy系统，从运动传感器变化中重建与脑电波相关的表征，从而推断用户的视觉刺激和大脑感知。

Result: 在多种VR设备上，BraVeSpy首次在元宇宙中实现感知图像重建（52.0%-67.2%准确率），在用户活动相关信息推断（指纹网站、应用、流媒体视频等）上超85.0%准确率，在用户去匿名化、眼动追踪和虚拟按键推断上超96.0%准确率。

Conclusion: VR头显的运动传感器可被利用来重建脑电波相关表征，揭示超出可观察行为的严重隐私泄露，且该方法优于现有仅能进行粗粒度可观察行为推断的 approaches。

Abstract: Metaverse devices, such as virtual reality (VR), have seen substantial development and widespread applications in numerous areas. Although recent studies have revealed privacy leakages in VR, these vulnerabilities were limited in the scope of observable behaviors in virtual scenes (e.g., what a user is seeing). In this work, we uncover the feasibility of going beyond the scope of observable user behaviors to unobservable brain EEG-correlated representations (e.g., what a user is perceiving) by leveraging unrestricted motion sensors in VR headsets to reconstruct brain EEG signals, a seemingly neglected but promising vector. The insight is that the inbuilt motion sensors (e.g., accelerometers) in the VR headset can capture subtle vibrations induced by pupillary responses, which are highly correlated with users' visual stimuli and in-brain perceptions.
  Therefore, we design and implement BraVeSpy to systematically investigate and demonstrate the feasibility of this severe privacy leakage originating from brain EEG-correlated representations reconstructed from variations of inbuilt motion sensors. Our extensive evaluation results from different VR devices show that BraVeSpy, for the first time in the Metaverse, can reveal unobservable privacy, where we successfully unveiled perceptive images in the brain with 52.0%-67.2% accuracy. In particular, we also find that BraVeSpy outperforms the current approaches that are limited to coarse-grained inference of observable behaviors and achieves over 85.0% accuracy in inferring user activity-related sensitive information, such as fingerprinting websites, apps, and streaming videos, and over 96.0% accuracy in user de-anonymization, gaze movement tracking, and virtual keystroke inference.

</details>


### [86] [A Deployment-Oriented Framework for Explainable AI-Assisted eBPF/XDP Mitigation at the IoT Edge](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.10508)
*Abdurrahman Tolay*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 本文提出了一个基于Linux的IoT边缘网关概念框架，该框架结合了资源感知的流级AI风险评分、事件级可解释性，以及通过eBPF/XDP实现的有限缓解措施。控制器执行可逆、时间受限且带有关键设备保护机制的操作，实现用户空间与内核空间的职责分离，并定义了未来硬件感知的评估路径。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有IoT部署中，基于签名和阈值的控制机制在动态网络中作为独立手段存在不足，而离线AI基准性能无法反映实际部署的可操作性。因此需要一种结合资源感知AI、可解释性和轻量级内核级响应的混合方法。

Method: 提出概念框架，采用用户空间与内核空间分离的架构：用户空间负责复杂推理和策略控制，内核空间通过eBPF/XDP执行简洁的数据包处理决策。框架包含资源感知的流级AI风险评分、事件级可解释性、可逆时间受限的缓解措施，以及结构化日志记录。

Result: 本文未报告新的实验结果，而是提出了概念框架、研究议程以及未来硬件感知的评估路径，涵盖检测质量、资源成本、响应时序、回滚行为和合法流量保留等维度。

Conclusion: 该框架旨在弥补现有IoT安全机制（签名/阈值检测、离线AI）的不足，通过结合AI风险评分、可解释性和eBPF/XDP轻量级内核响应，实现更适用于动态IoT网络的安全网关方案。

Abstract: Internet of Things (IoT) deployments combine heterogeneous, resource-constrained devices with weak security configurations, exposed services, limited logging, patching constraints, and long lifecycles. Signature- and threshold-based controls remain useful baselines, but they are insufficient as standalone mechanisms in dynamic IoT networks. Likewise, offline artificial intelligence (AI) benchmark performance alone does not establish operational deployability. This article presents a conceptual framework and research agenda for a Linux-based IoT edge gateway that combines resource-aware flow-level AI-assisted risk scoring, event-level explainability, and bounded mitigation through eBPF/XDP. The controller applies reversible, time-limited actions subject to critical-device safeguards, updates packet-level enforcement state, and records structured logs. The architecture separates complex reasoning and policy control in user space from concise packet-handling decisions in the kernel. It also defines a future hardware-aware evaluation pathway covering detection quality, resource cost, response timing, rollback behaviour, and legitimate-traffic preservation. The paper does not report new experimental meas

</details>


### [87] [Assessing Automated Prompt Injection Attacks in Agentic Environments](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.10525)
*David Hofer,Edoardo Debenedetti,Florian Tramèr*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 对LLM代理的自动提示注入攻击进行了全面实证评估，发现黑盒优化（TAP）显著优于白盒梯度方法（GCG），攻击有效性依赖于攻击模型的能力和安全对齐，任务通用攻击可跨任务和领域迁移，但无法从开源模型迁移到前沿模型。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 间接提示注入对处理不可信外部数据的LLM代理构成严重威胁，但自动化攻击方法在真实代理场景中尚未得到充分探索。

Method: 在AgentDojo框架下，将白盒（GCG）和黑盒（TAP）自动化提示注入方法适配到代理场景，跨4个领域80个任务对，对多个模型进行全面实证评估。

Result: 黑盒优化（TAP）显著优于梯度方法（GCG），后者在有限计算预算下优化不稳定；TAP有效性依赖于攻击模型的能力和安全对齐；任务通用攻击可有效迁移到新任务和域外分布，但从开源模型优化的攻击无法迁移到GPT-5等前沿模型。

Conclusion: 自动化提示注入是一种可信但模型依赖的威胁，实现模型无关的利用仍存在显著障碍。

Abstract: Indirect prompt injection poses a critical threat to LLM agents that interact with untrusted external data, yet automated attack methods--proven effective for jailbreaking--remain underexplored in realistic agentic settings. We present a comprehensive empirical evaluation of automated prompt injection attacks against LLM agents, adapting both white-box (GCG) and black-box (TAP) methods to the agentic setting within the AgentDojo framework. We evaluate across 80 task pairs spanning four domains and multiple models, and find that black-box optimization substantially outperforms gradient-based methods, a gap we attribute to GCG's optimization instability under reasonable compute budgets. We also find that TAP's effectiveness depends on the attacker model, as both general capability and safety tuning affect attack success--stronger models produce more effective injections, while safety-tuned attackers can refuse to generate adversarial prompts. Task-universal attacks transfer effectively to unseen tasks and out-of-distribution domains, but attacks optimized on smaller open-source models do not transfer to frontier models like GPT-5. These findings highlight automated prompt injection as a credible but model-dependent threat, with significant barriers remaining for model-agnostic exploitation.

</details>


### [88] [A Hybrid Edge-Cloud Architecture for Low-Latency Entitlement Verification in Resource-Constrained Devices](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.10536)
*Pravin Nagare,Aditya Sabbineni,Devendra Dahiphale,Faiz Gouri,Pratik Thantharate*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 提出混合边缘-云 entitlement 框架，通过本地缓存层和 AEC-PR 算法将授权延迟从 422.8ms 降低至 18.4ms（降幅 95.6%），并利用确定性 Ed25519 运算和 TEE 隔离缓解侧信道风险。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: OTT 平台中基于同步云校验的 entitlement 和身份验证架构引入显著延迟，尤其在资源受限的消费电子设备上影响用户体验。

Method: 在设备中间件中实现安全的本地缓存层，提出自适应 entitlement 缓存与主动刷新（AEC-PR）算法，将用户交互与后端网络延迟解耦；采用确定性 Ed25519 算法和 TEE 隔离进行本地化密码学验证。

Result: 在 ARM Cortex-A 系列硬件上，授权延迟从平均 422.8ms 降至 18.4ms（降低 95.6%），同时有效缓解了实现层面的侧信道风险。

Conclusion: 混合边缘-云 entitlement 框架能显著降低授权延迟，提升用户体验，并具备安全性的保证。

Abstract: As digital media consumption shifts toward large-scale Over-the-Top (OTT) platforms, the efficiency of the control plane, specifically entitlement and identity verification, has become a critical factor in user experience. Current architectures often rely on synchronous cloud-tethered validation flows that introduce significant latency, especially on resource-constrained consumer electronics. This paper proposes a Hybrid Edge-Cloud Entitlement Framework designed to minimize user-perceived friction. By implementing a secure, local caching layer within device middleware and utilizing an Adaptive Entitlement Cache with Proactive Refresh (AEC-PR) algorithm, we decouple the user interaction from backend network variability. We evaluate the performance on ARM Cortex-A series hardware, demonstrating that localized cryptographic verification reduces authorization latency from a mean of 422.8ms to 18.4ms (a 95.6% reduction) while mitigating implementation-level side-channel risks through deterministic Ed25519 arithmetic and TEE isolation.

</details>


### [89] [From Data Heterogeneity to Convergence: A Data-Centric Review of Federated Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.10595)
*Huong Nguyen,Mickaël Bettinelli,Amirhossein Ghaffari,Alexandre Benoit,Hong-Tri Nguyen,Susanna Pirttikangas,Lauri Lovén*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 本文从数据视角全面综述联邦学习中的挑战，分析了非独立同分布数据对收敛的影响等级、实验数据拆分实践与真实现象的关联及伪影、数据相关漏洞与防御机制对收敛的影响，是首个提供数据的完整理解的联邦学习综述。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有联邦学习综述多聚焦于通用基础、安全实践、机遇挑战和应用，很少从数据视角出发，缺乏对数据属性、拆分协议和防御机制与收敛速度及稳定性之间关系的系统综合。

Method: 1) 将非IID数据分解为可量化特征，按对收敛的影响程度（强/中/轻）排序并解释机制；2) 将实验数据拆分实践与真实现象关联，揭示引入的伪影及其对精度的影响；3) 分析数据相关漏洞及防御对收敛的影响，报告清洁与对抗条件下的性能，明确收敛-鲁棒性权衡。

Result: 首个提供联邦学习中数据相关挑战完整理解的综述，为每个关注点提炼了清晰的要点，为实践者设计具有可预测收敛和稳定性的系统提供了可操作的指导。

Conclusion: 该综述填补了联邦学习领域缺乏数据视角系统分析的空白，通过量化非IID影响、揭示拆分伪影、分析防御与收敛的权衡，为联邦学习的实践应用提供了有价值的指导。

Abstract: Federated Learning (FL) has emerged as a promising solution for data hunger in centralized learning. This paradigm enables privacy with multiple clients to train a shared-task model collaboratively without exposing their local data. While being a key component in any learning system, data is also a primary source of vulnerabilities and challenges, and a major determinant of a stable and well-converged training. Existing FL reviews describe general foundations, security practices, opportunities, challenges, and applications, without delving into diverse aspects of data and considering problems from the data perspective. They rarely provide a data-lens synthesis that links concrete data properties, split protocols, and defenses to convergence speed and stability. This survey fills that gap with three advances. First, we analyze non-IID into measurable traits and rank their influence on convergence as strong, medium, or light, explaining the mechanisms behind each and reconciling evidence across images, texts, and graphs. Second, we connect experimental splitting practices to the real phenomena they emulate, expose the artifacts they introduce, and show how those artifacts affect target accuracy. Third, we analyze how data-related vulnerabilities and their proposed defenses affect convergence, reporting performance under clean and adversarial conditions to make the convergence-robustness trade-off explicit. To our knowledge, this is the first survey to provide a complete understanding of data-related challenges that govern FL. With clear takeaways distilled for each concern, our work serves as actionable guidance, helping practitioners design their system with predictable convergence and stability.

</details>


### [90] [Two-Way Confidential VMs (2cVM): Collaborative Confidential Computing for Mutually Distrustful Parties](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.10615)
*Jordi Thijsman,Merlijn Sebrechts,Stefan Lefever,Filip De Turck,Bruno Volckaert*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 2cVM是一种双层机密虚拟机架构，结合硬件可信执行环境（AMD SEV-SNP）和WebAssembly组件模型的细粒度沙箱隔离，通过不可变的Commitment Manifest管理多方协作计算中的数据和代码隐私保护，实验表明其性能开销从可忽略到约2倍，取决于内存访问模式。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 多方协作计算需要在保护敏感数据和专有代码的同时进行处理，现有的全同态加密和安全多方计算虽然能提供强机密性但对通用工作负载计算开销极大，缺乏实用的解决方案。

Method: 提出2cVM双层架构：底层使用硬件可信执行环境（AMD SEV-SNP）保护虚拟机内存，上层使用WebAssembly组件模型实现参与者代码间的细粒度沙箱隔离；通过Commitment Manifest（声明参与者、组件组成、数据通道和授权输出）锁定到VM并纳入远程证明，使策略不可变且可独立验证。

Result: 在商用硬件上对四类基准测试的评估表明，两层隔离的开销不是线性累加的：一旦工作负载在WebAssembly沙箱内执行，启用硬件内存保护的边际开销很小。根据内存访问模式，开销从顺序工作负载的可忽略到不规则指针追踪模式的约2倍不等。

Conclusion: 2cVM为隐私保护的协作计算提供了实用且可验证的基础，其双层隔离架构在安全性与性能之间取得了平衡。

Abstract: Collaborative computation across organizations is often constrained by the need to process sensitive data and proprietary code without exposing them to untrusted infrastructure or participants. Cryptographic approaches such as fully homomorphic encryption and secure multi-party computation provide strong confidentiality but remain impractical for general workloads due to their extreme computational cost. We present the Two-Way Confidential Virtual Machine (2cVM), a two-layer architecture that pairs a hardware trusted execution environment with an intra-workload isolation layer. Unlike regular Confidential Virtual Machines, 2cVM enforces mutual isolation between co-resident workloads, ensuring that participants retain control over their data and code. All computation in 2cVM is governed by a Commitment Manifest that enumerates participants, component composition, permitted data channels, and authorized outputs; the manifest is locked to the VM and incorporated into attestation evidence, making the policy immutable and independently verifiable throughout the VM's lifetime. A proof-of-concept realization combines AMD SEV-SNP for hardware protection with the WebAssembly Component Model for fine-grained sandboxing of participant code. Evaluation on commodity hardware across four benchmark classes shows that the two isolation layers do not accumulate linearly: once a workload executes inside the WebAssembly sandbox, the marginal cost of enabling hardware memory protection is small. Overhead is workload-dependent, governed primarily by memory access pattern, ranging from negligible for sequential workloads to approximately 2x for irregular, pointer-chasing access patterns. These results indicate that 2cVM provides a practical and verifiable foundation for privacy-preserving collaborative computation.

</details>


### [91] [snaproot: Decentralized File Integrity Verification Using Blockchain-Anchored Cryptographic Hashing](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.10625)
*Arslan Brömme,Tarkan Yavas*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: snaproot 是一个基于 Solana 区块链的轻量级文件完整性验证系统，通过 SHA-256 哈希锚定实现去中心化、高效的文件存在证明与作者身份证明，并提出了四层信任架构与存在性/作者性证明的显式分离概念。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有数字内容完整性验证方案存在两难困境：集中式方案引入信任依赖和单点故障，去中心化存储系统则产生过高的资源开销。本文旨在设计一种轻量级、去中心化且资源高效的文件完整性验证方案。

Method: snaproot 采用 Haber 和 Stornetta 的哈希锚定范式，在 Solana 区块链上存储文件的 SHA-256 哈希值作为不可变参考记录。验证时重新计算哈希并与链上存储值进行二元比对。系统提出四层信任架构（三层已实现，一层前瞻性设计），并正式定义了存在性证明（与密钥无关）与作者身份证明（与密钥绑定）的分离机制。

Result: 在 Solana Devnet 上对 1 KB 到 500 MB 的文件进行了实证评估，基于 SHA-256 的第二原像抗性进行了形式化威胁建模与安全分析。与 OpenTimestamps、OriginStamp、Chainpoint 进行了对比定位。

Conclusion: 存在性证明与作者身份证明的显式分离是该系统的核心概念贡献，使得即使密钥丢失，存在性保证仍然有效，而保留密钥时可维持更强的作者身份主张。系统存在预注册操纵和 AI 生成内容方面的局限性。

Abstract: The rapid growth of digital content has made reliable integrity verification increasingly important. Existing solutions rely either on centralized authorities, which introduce trust dependencies and single points of failure, or on decentralized storage systems that incur prohibitive resource overhead. In this paper, we present snaproot, a lightweight system that implements the hash-anchoring paradigm of Haber and Stornetta on the Solana blockchain to provide efficient, decentralized file integrity verification. snaproot generates a SHA-256 hash of a file and stores it immutably on-chain as a permanent reference record. Verification is performed by recomputing the hash and comparing it to the stored value, yielding a deterministic binary outcome. We describe a four-tier trust architecture comprising three realized tiers and one prospective tier for long-term persistence beyond the lifetime of any single blockchain. We present a formal threat model, a security analysis grounded in the second-preimage resistance of SHA-256, and an empirical evaluation on Solana Devnet across file sizes from 1 KB to 500 MB. A central conceptual contribution is the explicit separation between existence proof, the key-independent claim that a file existed at a given time, and authorship proof, the key-dependent binding between a record and a specific wallet identity. This separation allows existence guarantees to survive key loss while preserving stronger authorship claims where keys are retained. We position snaproot against OpenTimestamps, OriginStamp, and Chainpoint and discuss limitations with respect to pre-registration manipulation and AI-generated content.

</details>


### [92] [Layer Order Semantics for Automata-Based Cybersecurity](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.10649)
*Faruk Alpay,Taylan Alpay*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 本文提出层序自动机（layer-order automaton）的有限状态语义，形式化描述分层流水线中证据变换顺序如何决定安全事实的可见性。以HTTP请求反同步为例，区分了完整轨迹识别、在线编辑、决策合成与忠实强制等概念，证明正则策略超出因果可见性边界后可识别但不可部署。该框架与有限输出确定性编辑自动机等价，同时保留层局部不变量，为审计、合成和比较分层强制流水线提供了自动机理论工具。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 分层网络安全流水线中，证据变换的顺序决定了哪些安全事实能被哪些层看到，这种顺序敏感性会导致同一攻击轨迹在不同流水线配置下被检测到或漏检。现有缺乏形式化工具来分析这种顺序导致的安全失效，因此需要建立一个自动机理论框架来系统描述和比较分层强制流水线的行为。

Method: 构建层序自动机（layer-order automaton），结合确定性序列安全变换器、证据标记和最终决策自动机。采用有限状态语义刻画变换顺序对可见性的影响，并通过HTTP请求反同步（CL.TE、TE.CL、TE.TE、HTTP/2降级）作为具体案例验证。引入上下文重排序同余类对组件排列进行分类。

Result: 区分了完整轨迹识别、在线编辑、决策合成与忠实强制；刻画忠实在线强制为因果可见性下的正则前缀闭包；证明正则策略超出该边界后可识别但不可部署；框架与有限输出确定性编辑自动机等价，同时保留标记出生、标记存活和重排序敏感可见性等局部不变量。具体解析器对语义识别出四种被禁止的标记因子。

Conclusion: 给出了顺序敏感安全失效的自动机理论解释，为审计、合成和比较分层强制流水线提供了组合式词汇表，有助于系统化分析和设计安全流水线。

Abstract: Layered cybersecurity pipelines transform evidence before they decide on it, and the order of those transformations determines which security facts become visible to which layer. This paper gives layer order a finite-state semantics built from a layer-order automaton, deterministic sequential security transducers, evidence markers, and a final decision automaton. The worked case is HTTP request desynchronization: front-end and back-end processors compute incompatible request boundaries, and the same trace is detected or missed according to whether framing evidence reaches the parser-differential layer before it commits. The results separate completed-trace recognition, online editing, decision synthesis, and faithful enforcement; characterize faithful online enforcement as the regular prefix-closed case under causal visibility; and show that regular policies beyond that boundary remain recognizable without becoming deployable enforcers. The framework is monolithically equivalent to finite-output deterministic edit automata, while preserving layer-local invariants such as marker birth, marker survival, and reorder-sensitive visibility. A concrete parser-pair semantics identifies the forbidden marker factor with CL.TE, TE.CL, TE.TE, and HTTP/2-downgrade boundary disagreement under the stated abstraction, and a contextual reorder congruence classifies which component permutations induce the same decision language. The result is an automata-theoretic account of order-sensitive security failures and a compositional vocabulary for auditing, synthesizing, and comparing layered enforcement pipelines.

</details>


### [93] [Post-Quantum Secure Federated DeFi for Inclusive Banking](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.10658)
*Swati Sachan,Dale Fickett,Richard Buchinger,Theo Miller*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 本文提出了一个后量子安全的联邦DeFi框架，利用基于格的完全同态加密（FHE）实现多银行间的加密协作，并结合NASA-IBM Prithvi地理空间基础模型生成的可验证证据，在保护隐私的前提下改善缺乏金融历史记录的借款人的信贷包容性，并在弗吉尼亚州农村贷款决策中进行了测试。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 后量子计算时代对现有密码学原语构成威胁，亟需开发抗量子攻击的加密金融系统；同时，传统金融体系中缺乏信用记录的个体难以获得贷款服务，需要一种隐私保护且包容性强的跨银行协作方案。

Method: 采用基于格的完全同态加密（FHE）构建虚拟服务器，多银行以加密形式输入本地概率评估数据；融合NASA-IBM Prithvi地理空间基础模型生成的可验证证据与专家信念；利用去中心化技术确保加密数据交换的防篡改和可审计性。

Result: 在弗吉尼亚州农村借款人的农业贷款决策场景中进行了实验验证，证明了该框架在保护隐私、抗量子安全和提升信贷包容性方面的可行性。

Conclusion: 该工作为后量子时代下DeFi系统的安全性提供了可行路径，同时通过隐私保护的联邦协作机制有效解决了金融排斥问题，具有重要的实际应用价值。

Abstract: Recent advances in error-corrected qubits have accelerated the timeline for practical quantum computing. It poses a threat to cryptographic primitives used to secure financial systems, government infrastructure, communication networks, and DeFi (Decentralized Finance) ecosystems. This paper introduces a post-quantum secure federated DeFi framework that enables inter-bank collaboration to improve the inclusivity of individuals underserved by local lenders due to limited financial histories. Multiple banks contribute encrypted information batches to a virtual server, where lattice-based Fully Homomorphic Encryption (FHE) enables end-to-end homomorphic computation. The server fuses local data-driven probabilistic assessments, expert beliefs, and verifiable evidence generated by the NASA-IBM Prithvi Geospatial Foundation Model (GFM), in encrypted format. Decentralized technologies are employed to ensure tamper-proof evidence and auditable accountability for all encrypted data exchanges between institutions and the server. The framework is tested on agricultural lending decisions for rural borrowers in Virginia.

</details>


### [94] [Do LLMsMakeNeural Distinguishers Wise?](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.10692)
*Tatsuya Sakagami,Masashi Hisai,Naoto Yanai*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 本文首次探讨将大型语言模型（LLM）用于神经区分器（一种对称密钥密码分析方法），通过在SPECK-32/64上进行实验，发现LLM相比ResNet没有带来可观察的性能提升；高轮次下差异选择对LLM和ResNet均无效；仅引入XOR操作结果能显著提升LLM神经区分器性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有神经区分器工作未探索过使用LLM进行密码分析，作者希望探究LLM是否能增强神经区分器的性能。

Method: 设计基于提示（prompt）的LLM神经区分器，在SPECK-32/64密码算法上进行大量实验，并与现有工作中ResNet的结果进行对比分析。

Result: 1) LLM相比ResNet在神经区分器性能上没有可观察的提升；2) 高轮次下差异选择对LLM和ResNet均失效；3) 仅将XOR操作结果加入提示设计可显著提升LLM神经区分器性能。

Conclusion: LLM在神经区分器任务上并未超越传统深度学习模型（ResNet），但通过合理设计提示（尤其是引入XOR操作结果）仍可提升性能，为未来基于LLM的密码分析提供了实证基础和方向。

Abstract: Neural distinguishers are a cryptanalysis method for symmetric-key cryptography that trains machine learning models on pairs of plaintexts and ciphertexts with specific differences in order to recover a secret key. To the best of our knowledge, no existing work has explored the use of large language models (LLMs) for neural distinguishers. In this paper, we propose LLM-based neural distinguishers through a prompt design and conduct extensive experiments with them on SPECK-32/64 to investigate whether LLMs can strengthen neural distinguishers. We then found three key insights. First, by comparing the results of LLM-based neural distinguishers with ResNet in the existing work, we demonstrate that LLMs provide no observable improvement in the performance of neural distinguishers. Second, we confirm that, at high rounds, the choice of differences is no longer effective for LLM-based neural distinguishers as well as ResNet. Third, we show that the performance of LLM-based neural distinguishers can be significantly improved by incorporating only the XOR operation results as a prompt design.

</details>


### [95] [Fingerprinting All AI Cluster I/O Without Mutually Trusted Processors](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.10724)
*Naci Cankaya,Jakub Kryś,Jonathan Ng,Luke Marks,Felix Krückel*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 提出了一种为AI数据中心建立密码学承诺的验证基础设施架构，通过部署网络分流器计算所有进出数据的哈希值，并设计安全网关设备消除隐蔽信道，使隐蔽外泄未披露工作负载结果不可行，无需双方信任的处理器，开发成本相当于一个小型工程师团队数月的工作。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 为应对未来可能达成的国际人工智能协议，亟需建立AI数据中心的验证基础设施，确保无法通过隐蔽信道外泄未披露工作负载的结果。

Method: 在网络线缆上部署分流器计算所有数据哈希值，通过密码学承诺实现事后可挑战的验证；设计安全网关设备消除模拟侧信道、时序侧信道和网络协议头部隐写术等隐蔽信道；采用无源光纤分路器和抛硬币协议进行随机数生成，无需双方信任的处理器。

Result: 提出了Secure Gateway Device的完整架构，能够在密码学层面使通过被 tapped 线缆隐蔽外泄工作负载结果不可行，开发成本和物料清单相对较低。

Conclusion: 该架构为AI数据中心的国际合规验证提供了可行的技术方案，兼顾安全性与隐私保护，且经济可承受。

Abstract: In preparation for potential international agreements on artificial intelligence, the development of verification infrastructure for AI data centres is vital. We propose a method for cryptographically committing all information entering and leaving a data centre: Hashes are computed by network taps placed on all the information-carrying wires between the cluster and the outside world, enabling an auditor to retroactively challenge the preimage data to be sent to a privacy-preserving verification facility performing compliance checks. Our goal is to make it infeasible to covertly exfiltrate the results of undisclosed workloads in the cluster through the tapped wires. To this end, we specify the architecture of a ``Secure Gateway Device'', which handles the erasure of covert channels that post-hoc verification on hashed data cannot address: analogue and timing side-channels, as well as steganography in network protocol headers. The architecture eliminates the need for any processors trusted by both the Prover and the Verifier, leveraging passive optical fibre splitters and coin-flip protocols for random number generation where needed. We expect development costs of a demonstration device to be roughly equivalent to the cost of a small team of engineers for a few months, with a comparatively small bill of materials.

</details>


### [96] [MemVenom: Triggered Poisoning of Multimodal Memories in Web Agents](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.10742)
*Yv Zhang,Hao Sun,Hao Fang,Kuofeng Gao,Fan Mo,Bin Chen,Shu-Tao Xia,Yaowei Wang*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 本文提出MemVenom，一种针对多模态Web智能体图结构外部记忆的黑盒投毒攻击框架，通过文本-图像协同证据实现持久、可重用的恶意记忆注入，在多个框架和视觉语言模型上实现高达99.15%的攻击成功率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 外部记忆已成为现代Web智能体的核心组件，但恶意内容注入记忆后可被持久召回并反复影响智能体行为。现有攻击局限于提示词或纯文本记忆，忽视了多模态记忆投毒这一实用攻击面。

Method: 提出MemVenom统一黑盒攻击框架，采用两阶段设计：(1) 触发条件驱动的检索攻击，确保恶意记忆被高概率召回；(2) 检索后攻击诱导，利用对抗扰动和隐蔽OCR注入覆盖原始用户目标。

Result: 在GPT-5系列Web智能体上攻击成功率高达99.15%，对良性性能影响极小，且在不同架构和模型规模间有效迁移。

Conclusion: MemVenom揭示了多模态记忆投毒这一被忽视的安全漏洞，实现了无需修改模型参数或重新优化恶意任务的持久、可重用、目标无关攻击。

Abstract: External memory has become a core component of modern web agents, enabling long-horizon reasoning through the retrieval of past experiences. However, this paradigm introduces a critical vulnerability: malicious content injected into memory can be persistently recalled and repeatedly influence agent behavior. In this work, we identify and systematically study multimodal memory poisoning, an overlooked yet practical attack surface in web-agent systems. We propose MemVenom, a unified black-box attack framework that poisons graph-structured external memory with coordinated text-image evidence. Our method consists of a two-stage design: (1) a trigger-conditioned retrieval attack that ensures high-probability recall of malicious memory, and (2) a post-retrieval attack induction that leverages adversarial perturbations and stealthy OCR injection to override the original user objective. Unlike prior attacks that operate on prompts or text-only memory, our approach enables persistent, reusable, and goal-agnostic attacks without modifying model parameters or re-optimizing malicious tasks. Experiments across multiple web-agent frameworks and vision-language models demonstrate that MemVenom achieves strong end-to-end attack success with minimal impact on benign performance, reaching up to 99.15% on GPT-5-family web agents, while transferring effectively across architectures and model scales.

</details>


### [97] [Toward Secure LLM Agents: Threat Surfaces, Attacks, Defenses, and Evaluation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.10749)
*Yuchen Ling,Shengcheng Yu,Zhenyu Chen,Chunrong Fang*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 本文基于247篇文献，通过生命周期、系统导向的框架系统梳理了LLM智能体安全研究，涵盖威胁建模、攻击面、防御策略与评估方法，指出当前研究聚焦于提示注入和工具劫持，而持久状态破坏与多智能体传播正在成为新兴核心问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: LLM智能体正从对话界面转向可规划、调用工具、维护记忆并在外部环境中行动的软件组件，这一转变从根本上改变了安全风险的性质——不再局限于不安全的文本生成，而是扩展到控制流劫持、权限滥用、状态污染、信息泄露和有害外部行为。然而，该领域研究虽快速扩展却高度碎片化，缺乏统一的系统性分析框架。

Method: 采用生命周期驱动、系统导向的分析框架，围绕信息流、委派权限和持久状态三个核心维度建模智能体安全，系统检索并综合了247篇论文，从威胁建模、攻击面与攻击类型、防御策略及其权衡、安全评估方法四个问题维度组织文献。

Result: 提示注入和工具介导的控制流劫持仍主导该领域；持久状态破坏和多智能体传播正成为新兴核心关切；现有防御提供了有用构建块但可组合性弱；现有基准仍低估长时域、有状态及部署敏感的风险。

Conclusion: 构建安全的LLM智能体需要明确的信任边界、有原则的权限控制、可溯源的状态管理，以及与真实运行环境对齐的评估实践。

Abstract: Large language model (LLM) agents are rapidly moving from conversational interfaces to software components that plan, invoke tools, maintain memory, and act on external environments. This transition changes the nature of security risk. In agentic settings, failures are no longer limited to unsafe text generation. Untrusted content may redirect control flow, misuse tool privileges, corrupt persistent state, leak sensitive information, or trigger harmful external actions. At the same time, research on LLM agent security is expanding quickly but remains fragmented across attack families, defense layers, application domains, and evaluation settings. This paper synthesizes 247 papers through a lifecycle-based, systems-oriented framework that models agent security around the interaction of information flow, delegated authority, and persistent state. We organize the literature around four questions: how LLM agent security should be modeled, which threat surfaces and attack families dominate, what defenses have been proposed and with what tradeoffs, and how security claims are evaluated. We find that prompt injection and tool-mediated control-flow hijacking still dominate the field, while persistent state corruption and multi-agent propagation are becoming central emerging concerns. We further find that current defenses provide useful building blocks but remain weakly compositional, and that existing benchmarks still underrepresent long-horizon, stateful, and deployment-sensitive risks. We argue that secure LLM agents require explicit trust boundaries, principled privilege control, provenance-aware state management, and evaluation practices aligned with realistic operational settings.

</details>


### [98] [A Bayesian Network Approach for Enhancing Security-Focused Decision Support Systems](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.10782)
*Carolina Fernández-Martínez,Shuaib Siddiqui,Vanesa Daza*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种基于贝叶斯网络的决策支持系统（DSS），帮助网络基础设施运维人员根据安全三元组（机密性、完整性、可用性）的高层需求，自动推荐合适的安全工具与机制。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当前开源网络环境中异构技术栈的广泛采用带来了互操作性和高级功能等好处，但组件数量的不断增加要求运维人员掌握跨领域、跨工具的多学科知识，运维负担日益加重。为减轻这一负担，需要一种系统化的决策支持方法。

Method: 提出一个决策支持系统（DSS）框架，捕获终端用户对不同领域安全三元组的高层需求，利用贝叶斯网络（BN）模型进行推理，输出最适合的安全工具/机制。系统设计注重可理解性和可扩展性，支持不同需求场景和模型。

Result: 在时间性能和预测准确性两方面对系统进行了评估。

Conclusion: 该DSS能够有效降低运维人员在不同安全域中选择合适安全工具的认知负担，提供了一个可理解、可扩展的自动化决策框架。

Abstract: The adoption and integration of heterogeneous stacks in most of today's open-source based networks brings clear benefits like interoperability and availability of advanced features. Yet, on the other hand the increasing number of interconnecting components and moving parts requires maintaining an ever increasing base of interdisciplinary knowledge of different tools in different domains to ensure proper operation. To alleviate such efforts, this work proposes a Decision Support System (DSS) to guide infrastructure operators through the selection of security approaches (e.g. tools) to adopt in their environments. This framework easily captures the end-user high-level requirements on the security triad for different domains and runs inference on the designated models to provide the identified tools (security mechanisms) that better serve such needs. The presented DSS aims at delivering an understandable and extensible framework to accommodate varying requirements and Bayesian Network (BN) models. The architecture and modelling of the system are proposed, aligned with its theoretical framework. Its performance is evaluated in terms of time and prediction accuracy.

</details>


### [99] [RedAct: Redacting Agent Capability Traces for Procedural Skill Protection](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.10813)
*Shuwen Xu,Zhitao He,Yi R.,Fung*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 本文构建了CapTraceBench基准（75个长时任务、154个技能）并提出RedAct框架，通过定位关键信息、重写痕迹并嵌入水印，将技能迁移率从44.7-67.1%降至基线以下，同时保持审计证据完整性，水印检测率达93.6-100%，虚警率≤1.9%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 执行痕迹虽能帮助用户观察智能体行为、诊断故障和确保问责，但其丰富的程序细节（工具调用、中间决策、错误恢复逻辑）可能泄露私有过程技能，使下游方法无需访问模型权重或技能文件即可恢复关键公式、阈值和策略。现有研究缺乏对此风险的量化评估与保护方法。

Method: 1) 构建CapTraceBench基准，涵盖7个领域75个长时任务和154个精细技能；2) 提出RedAct框架，包含三个组件：定位受保护关键信息、重写痕迹同时保留验证器关键证据、嵌入行为水印用于下游溯源分析。

Result: RedAct将归一化技能迁移率（NST）从原始痕迹的44.7-67.1%降至无技能基线以下，同时保留审计证据；行为水印的真实检测率达93.6-100%，虚警率最高为1.9%。

Conclusion: 公共智能体痕迹应被视为安全接口，选择性编辑可以在不移除审计证据的情况下减少过程能力泄露。

Abstract: Users rely on execution traces to observe agent behavior, diagnose failures, and ensure accountability. These traces contain rich procedural detail, including tool invocations, intermediate decisions, and error-recovery logic. Yet this detail can expose private procedural skills, allowing downstream methods to recover key formulas, thresholds, and strategies without access to model weights or skill files. To quantify this risk and evaluate protection, we construct \textsc{CapTraceBench}, a benchmark of 75 specialized long-horizon tasks and 154 curated skills across seven domains. We also introduce \textsc{RedAct} https://github.com/XuShuwenn/RedAct, a protected trace release framework that localizes protected key information, rewrites traces while preserving verifier-critical evidence, and embeds behavioral watermarks for downstream provenance analysis. Across representative trace reuse methods, \textsc{RedAct} reduces normalized skill transfer (NST) from 44.7--67.1\% on raw traces to below the no-skill baseline, while preserving audit evidence. Its standalone behavioral watermarks reach 93.6--100.0\% true detection with a false alarm rate of at most 1.9\%. These results frame public agent traces as security interfaces and show that selective redaction can reduce procedural capability leakage without removing audit evidence.

</details>


### [100] [Securing Code Understanding: Detecting Natural Backdoor Vulnerability in Code Language Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.10846)
*Yuchen Chen,Weisong Sun,Haocheng Huang,Yuan Xiao,Chunrong Fang,Yiran Zhang,Tingting Xu,Zhenpeng Chen,An Guo,Peizhuo Lv,Xiaofang Zhang,Zhenyu Chen,Yang Liu,Baowen Xu*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 本文系统研究了代码语言模型（CodeLMs）中自然后门漏洞的普遍性、特性、传递性、成因、防御及检测方法，提出了新型检测工具ScanNBT。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 尽管自然后门在常规深度学习模型中已被发现且威胁严重，但代码语言模型中自然后门漏洞的安全隐患尚不明确，亟需系统性研究。

Method: 在44个场景、多种模型架构和代码智能任务上进行实证研究，从模型/参数层级对比注入后门与自然后门差异，分析数据集、架构、知识三方面的可传递性，从训练数据和训练过程两方面探究成因，评估预训练、训练中、训练后三类防御技术，并提出ScanNBT检测方法。

Result: 自然后门在CodeLMs中普遍存在且为固有特性；自然后门与注入后门在模型和参数层面存在差异；自然后门在数据集、模型架构和共享知识间具有可传递性；现有防御技术效果有限；ScanNBT能更全面地检测自然后门漏洞。

Conclusion: 自然后门是CodeLMs固有且普遍的安全威胁，研究结果有助于理解其机理并提升代码语言模型的后门防御能力。

Abstract: Code Language Models (CodeLMs) have become integral to software engineering, significantly advancing code intelligence tasks. However, their widespread adoption has raised critical security concerns, particularly regarding susceptibility to backdoor attacks. Recent studies have uncovered naturally occurring backdoors, referred to as natural backdoors, in normally trained deep learning models. Despite posing threats as serious as those introduced through data poisoning, security implications of natural backdoor vulnerabilities in CodeLMs remain poorly understood.
  In this paper, we conduct a thorough empirical study of natural backdoor vulnerabilities in CodeLMs across various model architectures and code intelligence tasks. Specifically, we examine potential natural backdoor vulnerabilities across 44 scenarios, demonstrating that natural backdoors are prevalent and intrinsic to CodeLMs. We reveal differences between injected and natural backdoor vulnerabilities at both the model and parameter levels. We then analyze the transferability of natural backdoor vulnerabilities from three perspectives: datasets, model architectures, and shared knowledge. We further investigate the causes of natural backdoors from two aspects: training datasets and the model training procedure. We evaluate existing backdoor defense techniques, including pre-training, in-training, and post-training defenses, in mitigating natural backdoors. Finally, we propose ScanNBT, a novel detection method designed to improve comprehensive detection of natural backdoor vulnerabilities in CodeLMs. We aim for our findings to enhance understanding of these vulnerabilities and provide insights for strengthening CodeLM security against backdoor threats.

</details>


### [101] [Training LLMs to Enforce Multi-Level Instruction Hierarchies via Gravity-Weighted Direct Preference Optimization](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.10860)
*Lena S. Bolliger,Lena A. Jäger*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 本文针对大语言模型的结构性漏洞——所有token享有统一架构特权——提出k级指令层次问题，并实例化为k=5。作者提出GW-DPO（重力加权DPO）偏好优化目标，结合线性/双边调度、层次分隔符和指令段嵌入，在Llama-3.1-8B-Instruct上实现Pareto改进，提升了多层级优先级遵从率，同时将过度拒绝率降至标准DPO的一半。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: LLM在架构上赋予所有token同等的注意力特权，导致恶意提示注入可轻易劫持模型行为；同时模型缺乏原则性机制来解决合法但冲突的指令。现有训练方案仅形式化3-4级层次，将所有违规等同处理，且很少评估完整的层级配对交互。

Method: 形式化k级指令层次问题（k=5，得到10个配对优先级关系）；提出GW-DPO，其每样本偏移量根据冲突层级间的结构距离按线性或双边调度缩放（双边调度同时考虑特权差距与受害层特权）；结合层次分隔符（Chen et al., 2025）和指令段嵌入（ISE; Wu et al., 2025）。

Result: 在Llama-3.1-8B-Instruct上，采用双边调度的GW-DPO在宏观配对优先级遵从率上Pareto优于标准DPO和线性变体，过度拒绝率仅为标准DPO的一半。

Conclusion: 消融实验表明ISE充当拒绝阈值校准器；五级与三级训练对比体现了通用性-专业性的权衡。

Abstract: Production LLMs receive instructions from sources with very different levels of trust, yet attend to every token with uniform architectural privilege. This is the structural vulnerability that enables malicious prompt injections and, more broadly, leaves models without a principled way to resolve conflicts between legitimate but competing instructions. A common training-based response is to teach models an explicit instruction hierarchy; existing approaches, however, formalize hierarchies of only three or four levels, treat all violations as equally severe, and rarely evaluate the full set of pairwise level interactions. We formalize a k-level instruction hierarchy problem and instantiate it for k=5, yielding ten pairwise priority relations that a compliant model must enforce. We then introduce Gravity-Weighted DPO (GW-DPO), a preference-optimization objective whose per-sample offset scales with the structural distance between conflicting levels under a linear or bilateral schedule, the latter weighting severity by both the privilege gap and the privilege of the victim level. Combined with hierarchy-specific delimiter tokens (Chen et al., 2025) and Instructional Segment Embeddings (ISE; Wu et al., 2025), GW-DPO with the bilateral schedule Pareto-improves over standard DPO and the linear variant on Llama-3.1-8B-Instruct, raising macro pairwise priority adherence while keeping over-refusal at half the standard DPO rate. Ablations isolate ISE as a refusal-threshold calibrator and recast five- versus three-level training as a generality-specialization tradeoff.

</details>


### [102] [Comparative Analysis of Inference-Time Defense Methods for Multimodal Large Language Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.10904)
*Bulat Nutfullin,Vladimir Evgrafov,Dmitry Namiot*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 本文对多模态大语言模型（MLLMs）的七种安全基准、八种模型下的三种推理时防御方法进行了实证评估，发现无单一防御在所有场景中占优，组合防御会导致过度拒绝率飙升，而安全提示在保持实用性的同时提供适度安全增益；结论主张采用自适应防御选择策略。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 多模态大语言模型在安全关键应用中的视觉通道易受对抗性攻击，而现有的以文本为主的安全对齐无法充分防御；重新训练模型以应对每种新漏洞代价过高，因此需要探索推理时的轻量防御方案。

Method: 对InternVL和Qwen-VL家族中的8个模型，使用统一的代理分类器，在7个安全基准（涵盖4种攻击类别，总计9000个评估样本）上，对三种推理时防御方法及其组合进行了比较实证评估，并进行了初步白盒测试（n=20）。

Result: 1) 无单一防御在所有设定中占优；2) 直接组合防御导致良性查询的过度拒绝率飙升至97-100%，SmoothVLM单独达到99.2-100%；3) 安全提示保持低过度拒绝率（0.0-18.2%），同时提供中等安全增益；4) 不同攻击类别暴露不同弱点；5) 白盒测试中文本级防御抑制了PGD视觉攻击。

Conclusion: 推理时防御的效果高度依赖于模型基线和攻击类型，单一固定防御配置不可取，应采用自适应防御选择策略。

Abstract: Multimodal large language models (MLLMs) now appear in safety-critical applications, but the visual channel leaves them open to adversarial attacks that predominantly text-oriented safety alignment addresses only in part. Retraining a model for each new vulnerability class is usually too expensive to be practical. We report a comparative empirical evaluation of three inference-time defense methods and their combinations, run on eight models from the InternVL and Qwen-VL families across seven safety benchmarks that span four attack classes and total 9,000 evaluation samples. Every figure below comes from the same unified proxy classifier. Five findings emerge from the evaluation. First, within the evaluated models and benchmarks, no single defense dominates across all settings: what works depends on the model's baseline safety and on the attack type. Second, combining defenses directly drives benign-query over-refusal to 97-100% across all eight evaluated models, and SmoothVLM on its own reaches 99.2-100%. Third, a simple safety prompt keeps utility largely intact (0.0-18.2% over-refusal across all eight models, five of them below 7%, although two exceeded 15%) while still yielding moderate safety gains. Fourth, different attack classes expose different weaknesses across the evaluated setup, which is why multi-benchmark evaluation matters. Fifth, in a preliminary whitebox test on two models (n=20), text-level defenses suppressed a PGD visual attack that had succeeded without any defense: the defenses act at the output stage, where gradient optimization has limited direct leverage in the tested configuration. Read together, these results argue for adaptive defense selection rather than a single fixed defense configuration.

</details>


### [103] [Context-Based Adversarial Attacks on AI Code Generators: Vulnerability Analysis and Implications](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.10945)
*Walther A. Del Orbe,John D. Hastings,Varghese Vaidyan*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 本文系统研究了针对AI代码生成系统的上下文对抗攻击，发现通过策略性构造的上下文输入可使漏洞代码生成率提升10.7倍（从3.5%到37.4%），并提出了一个具有89.1%检测率、0.3%误报率和520ms延迟的双层防御框架。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: AI代码生成系统在软件开发中被广泛使用，但其在推理时存在安全漏洞，攻击者可能通过构造恶意上下文输入诱导模型生成可被利用的代码，亟需系统性的研究和防御方案。

Method: 通过2,800次控制实验，在CodeT5+、CodeLlama、GPT-3.5-Turbo和GPT-4四个模型上测试上下文对抗攻击（包括注释、文档、变量名等策略性输入），并提出双层防御框架。

Result: 对抗条件使漏洞生成率提升10.7倍（3.5%→37.4%），GPT-3.5-Turbo上直接指令攻击成功率100%，跨模型迁移率达60-100%；双层防御框架实现89.1%检测率、0.3%误报率和520ms延迟。

Conclusion: 上下文对抗攻击对主流代码生成模型构成严重威胁，且攻击具有高跨模型迁移性，表明这是系统性架构漏洞而非模型特有缺陷；提出的双层防御框架在实时性、准确率方面具备实际部署可行性。

Abstract: AI-powered code generation systems have transformed software development but introduce critical inference-time security vulnerabilities. This research presents a systematic investigation of context-based adversarial attacks, where strategically crafted contextual inputs, including comments, documentation, variable names, bias large language models toward generating exploitable code. Through 2,800 controlled experiments across CodeT5+, CodeLlama, GPT-3.5-Turbo, and GPT-4, we quantify attack effectiveness and defense mechanisms. Results demonstrate that adversarial conditions increase vulnerability generation 10.7x (from 3.5% to 37.4%), with direct instruction attacks achieving 100% success on GPT-3.5-Turbo. Cross-model transferability reaches 60-100%, indicating systemic architectural vulnerabilities rather than model-specific flaws. Our dual-layer defense framework achieves 89.1% detection rate with 0.3% false positives and 520ms latency, demonstrating practical feasibility for real-time deployment in development environments.

</details>


### [104] [Understanding and mitigating the risks of OpenClaw for non-technical users: A practical guide with Skill](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.11007)
*Junchang Zheng,Junfeng Tan,Jialiang Lin*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 本文面向非技术用户，识别了OpenClaw AI代理框架的七类核心风险，并提供了通俗易懂的解释、可操作的防御策略以及自动化安全配置的配套技能，旨在降低非技术用户使用智能代理的安全门槛。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有针对OpenClaw安全威胁的研究主要面向技术专家，而日益增长的非技术用户群体缺乏通俗易懂的安全指导，迫切需要降低风险的实用指南。

Method: 1) 识别并分类七类核心风险，用通俗语言解释；2) 为每类风险提炼清晰可操作的防御步骤；3) 开发配套的OpenClaw Skill自动化安全配置，减少人工干预。

Result: 提供了面向非技术用户的完整风险认知与防御方案，并通过自动化技能降低了安全防护的操作门槛。

Conclusion: 智能代理的安全防护不应是安全专家的专属领域，非技术用户通过简单实用的行动也能有效参与降低风险。

Abstract: OpenClaw has rapidly emerged as a transformative artificial intelligence (AI) agent framework, and its ability to autonomously execute complex, multi-step tasks has attracted an ever-growing and diverse user base. However, this capability comes with significant risks. While existing research has made important strides in characterizing these threats, such work is predominantly directed at technically sophisticated audiences. It remains largely inaccessible to non-technical users. This demographic now makes up an increasingly large and underserved portion of the community, yet it is these very users who most urgently need practical and straightforward guidance. In response, we bridge this gap through a series of interconnected efforts designed to lower the risk barrier for non-technical OpenClaw users. First, we identify and categorize seven core risks that OpenClaw users may encounter in daily usage, explaining each in plain language so that non-technical users can readily grasp the nature and potential consequences of these threats. Second, for each identified risk, we distill a set of corresponding defensive strategies into clear and actionable operational steps that are easy to follow. Third, to make protection even easier, we provide a companion OpenClaw Skill that automates key security configurations, enabling users to safeguard their systems with minimal manual intervention. Through this work, we demonstrate that safeguarding against the risks of intelligent agents need not be the exclusive domain of security experts, and that non-technical users can meaningfully participate in reducing these risks through simple, practical actions.

</details>


### [105] [When Discovery Outpaces Remediation: Modeling AI-Accelerated Vulnerability Discovery in Interconnected Systems](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.11022)
*Mohamamad Reza Faghani*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 本文提出一个基于排队论和网络理论的人工智能加速漏洞发现模型，分析在互联系统中，当漏洞发现速率超过修复能力时，积压会急剧增长，系统性风险非线性上升；在枢纽型拓扑中，网络分段比单纯提高修复速度更有效，而最佳防御策略是修复自动化与降低网络耦合相结合。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 先进AI系统能显著提高漏洞发现速率，但可能压垮修复管线并加速对手武器化，现有研究缺乏对这一过程的量化建模与风险分析。

Method: 建立加权依赖图模型，包含漏洞池补给、有限修复容量、分类退化、利用窗口压缩及动态传播等机制，推导积压稳定性条件，并通过仿真评估缓解策略。

Result: 当可操作的漏洞发现到达率超过修复吞吐量时，积压快速膨胀，系统性风险呈非线性增长；在枢纽主导拓扑中，网络分段比提升修复速度更能降低传播风险；修复自动化与降低网络耦合的组合策略效果最佳。

Conclusion: AI加速漏洞发现可能引发修复积压和系统性风险非线性增长，需结合修复自动化与网络分段等多层次防御策略。

Abstract: Advanced AI systems for code analysis, binary analysis, fuzzing orchestration, and penetration-test planningmay significantly increase the rate at which latent vulnerabilities are discovered. While improved discovery can benefit defenders, it can also overload remediation pipelines and accelerate adversarial weaponization. This paper develops a queueing and network-theoretic model of AI-accelerated vulnerability discovery in interconnected systems. We represent an enterprise as a weighted dependency graph with replenishing vulnerability pools, finite remediation capacity, triage degradation, exploit window compression, and dynamic compromise propagation. We derive stability conditions for vulnerability backlogs, formulate a dynamic coupling between unresolved backlog and cascade risk, and evaluate mitigation strategies through simulation. Results indicate that when actionable discovery arrivals exceed remediation throughput, backlogs grow rapidly and systemic risk increases nonlinearly. In hub-dominated topologies, segmentation can reduce propagated compromise more effectively than remediation speed alone, while the strongest defense combines remediation automation with reduced network coupling.

</details>


### [106] [Do Transformers Actually Help Intrusion Detection? A Temporal Sequence Evaluation on CIC-IDS2017](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.11098)
*Zach Moczkodan,Hany Ragab*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 本文系统研究了CIC-IDS2017数据集上的时序入侵检测方法，发现填充约定（padding convention）而非架构选择决定了Transformer的性能表现；在无泄漏的评估协议下，随机森林是最鲁棒的模型，而Transformer的误报率在传统协议下被严重低估。研究表明评估方法论（填充约定和数据集划分协议）对性能指标的影响远超模型架构选择本身。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有基于深度学习的网络入侵检测方法虽大量采用时序架构（RNN、Transformer）并在CIC-IDS2017上报告近乎完美的性能，但许多研究既未向时序模块提供真正的序列输入，也未在真实、无泄漏的条件下进行评估。这使得报告的性能提升究竟源自真正的序列建模能力还是评估漏洞，成为一个未解的问题。

Method: 将CIC-IDS2017重新构建为时序入侵检测任务，通过从网络会话中构建有序流序列，在随机划分、两种无泄漏划分以及填充方案消融实验下系统评估9种经典和深度学习架构。

Result: Transformer在真实序列（非填充）窗口下达到最高macro-F1（0.89）；在零填充+掩码评估下macro-F1显著下降0.24，而LSTM、GRU和1D-CNN保持稳定。在无泄漏组评估下，随机森林是最鲁棒的模型（+0.009），而Transformer的误报率从0.04%增长至2.7%（67倍增长），这在传统评估协议下是不可见的。

Conclusion: 评估方法论（填充约定和数据集划分协议）对性能指标的影响远超模型架构选择；广泛使用的随机划分+重复最后值填充可能高估模型鲁棒性高达0.24 macro-F1。建议未来IDS研究采用无泄漏划分、明确的填充披露和序列感知基准测试作为标准实践。

Abstract: Recent deep learning approaches for network intrusion detection increasingly incorporate temporal architectures such as recurrent networks and Transformers, often reporting near-perfect performance on CIC-IDS2017. However, many existing studies neither supply their temporal modules with genuine sequence inputs nor evaluate under realistic, leakage-free conditions, making it unclear whether reported gains arise from true sequence-modeling capability. In this work, we reformulate CIC-IDS2017 as a temporal intrusion-detection task by constructing ordered flow sequences from network conversations and benchmarking nine classical and deep learning architectures under a random split, two leakage-free splits, and a padding-scheme ablation. The central finding is that padding convention, not architecture, determines the Transformer's performance: on genuinely sequential (non-padded) windows the Transformer achieves the highest macro-F1 of any model in the experiment (0.89); under zero-pad+mask evaluation it drops markedly (-0.24 macro-F1), while LSTM, GRU, and 1D-CNN remain stable. Under leakage-free group evaluation the Random Forest is the most robust model (+0.009), while the Transformer's false-alarm rate grows from 0.04% to 2.7%, a 67-fold increase invisible under conventional protocols. These findings demonstrate that evaluation methodology -- specifically padding convention and split protocol -- has a larger effect on reported performance than architectural choice, and that widely used random splits with repeat-last padding can overestimate model robustness by up to 0.24 macro-F1. We advocate leakage-free splits, explicit padding disclosure, and sequence-aware benchmarking as standard practice in future IDS research. Code and implementation details are available at https://github.com/zachmocz/temporal-ids-bench.

</details>


### [107] [A Longitudinal Study of Recently Observed Malicious Domains: Characteristics, Infrastructure, and Abuse Patterns](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.11111)
*Fathima Mashood,Mohamed Nabeel*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 本文对2026年1月至5月间VirusTotal上约152万个恶意域名进行了纵向研究，分析了攻击者行为在时间分布、域名分类、年龄、注册商/TLD偏好、DNS查询量、托管基础设施、批量注册和品牌冒充八个维度的特征。关键发现包括：攻击者创建的域名大多是短期注册；少数注册商和TLD集中了大部分滥用；Cloudflare基础设施被广泛用于域名前置；批量注册事件普遍存在；WhatsApp和Google等全球品牌被大量冒充。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 理解恶意域名生态系统的行为模式，揭示攻击者如何利用域名基础设施进行恶意活动，为防御策略提供数据驱动的洞察。

Method: 从VirusTotal收集约152万个恶意域名（被至少5个扫描引擎检测到，首次出现于2026年1月至5月），结合WHOIS注册记录和被动DNS数据，从八个维度对恶意域名进行分析，并将数据集分为被攻陷域名和攻击者创建域名两类。

Result: 攻击者创建的域名占89.3%，多为短期注册；少量注册商和TLD集中了大部分滥用；Cloudflare基础设施被广泛用于域名前置；存在大量同一注册商单日注册数千域名的批量事件；WhatsApp和Google等品牌被严重冒充。

Conclusion: 恶意域名活动呈现高度集中化的特征，防御资源应聚焦于特定注册商、TLD和托管服务商，并关注批量注册和品牌冒充行为。研究数据集已公开共享以支持后续研究。

Abstract: We present a longitudinal study of approximately 1.52 million malicious domains observed on VirusTotal (VT) between January and May 2026. Domains were selected on the basis of detection by at least five independent VT scanning engines and a first-seen date within the study window. We group the dataset into compromised domains and attacker created domains, which account for approximately 89.3% of the dataset. Combining WHOIS registration records and passive DNS (PDNS) data with the VT dataset, we characterise attacker behaviour across eight dimensions: temporal distribution, compromisedvs.attack classification, domain age at first detection, registrar and TLD preferences, DNS query volume as a damage proxy, hosting infrastructure concentration (IP and ASN level), bulk registration patterns, and brand impersonation.
